We want f ∈[-1,1], mean zero, autocorr R_d high positive for d up to m_target. We can construct f as real part of a sum of complex exponentials with random phases? If we choose many frequencies with random phases, autocorr is sum cos with random phases; might have negative dips. But we can choose spectrum nonnegative (positive definite) by taking squared magnitude of a complex vector in Fourier domain: f = inverse DFT of |G|^2 (real nonnegative autocorr spectrum), scaled and centered. Then f is a positive definite function on the finite group; all cyclic autocorr nonnegative. But noncyclic? If we create length n sequence from a positive cyclic spectrum with zero mean, then its cyclic autocorr nonnegative. But cyclic mean zero implies sum over cyclic autocorr? Wait if spectrum a_0=0 and autocorr r_k≥0 for k=0..n-1, then sum r_k = n a_0? For cyclic autocorr, the mean of r_k over k equals n a_0^2? Let's derive: cyclic autocorr r_k = DFT^{-1}(a_k?) If a spectrum of f (Fourier coeff) maybe autocorr coefficients are |F|^2. If F[0]=0, autocorr C[k]=IDFT(|F|^2). The average C over k = |F|^2[0] = 0. So if all C[k]≥0 and C0>0, average >0, contradiction unless F0>0. Thus a zero-mean periodic sequence cannot have all cyclic autocorr nonnegative. Indeed. So positive spectrum with zero mean impossible. Need nonperiodic (one-sided) autocorr can be positive. But we can use a finite nonnegative measure on continuous frequencies whose sampled autocorr is positive for d up to n-1? The sum over d up to n-1 not constrained by periodic mean? Coordinate descent exact:
For each coordinate p, function f(x) = max_{l in Ls} (base_l + (x-old)*A_l - δ_{l=diag} (x-old)^2). We can minimize over [0,1] robustly. Since number of lines ~2n-1≤1000. We can solve via ternary? For fixed i, moving i to 0: base modifications Δi = (-om slice p + h slice). For each j, moving j to 1: Δj. We can evaluate max of c + Δi + Δj + cross corrections. Cross corrections at two points depend on i,j. For each i, we can precompute base_i = c + Δi. Then for all zeros j, need max_j. We can compute Δj vectors for many j at once? A matrix J (m_zero x L) where row j = +om[j:j+n] - h[st_j:st_j+n] plus diag -1? But c0 self? Let's derive binary_trial from two changes without copy:
For i 1->0: d_i=-1; linear changes: c[i:i+n] -= om; c[diag-i: diag-i+n] += h; c[diag] -=1.
For j 0->1: d_j=+1: c[j:j+n] += om; c[diag-j: diag-j+n] -= h; c[diag] -=1.
Cross: if changes overlap, the linear uses old om/h at changed positions; need corrections: at diag: -2 total. Also at lags i-j and j-i +1.
Matrix for fixed i: base = c + Δi (with diag -1). For each j in zeros excluding i (i is one, not zero), candidate = base + Δj + cross_j where Δj = +om[j:j+n] - h[diag-j:diag-j+n] -1 at diag; cross = +1 at diag+i-j and diag+j-i (but note base already includes old om/h at j? After scan, apply best improving move (score). Then recompute J as h changed. Repeat until no improving move or time. This deterministic best-swap local search can find strong binary minima. Let's implement, but careful memory/time.

Function binary_best_scan(h,n,deadline,max_passes=5, top_per_i=8):
   h binary, c, om.
   score=score_c(c); m=c.max()
   improved_pass=0
   while time<deadline and improved_pass<max_passes:
      moved=False
      zeros = np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
      ones = np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]
      if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
      # Build J matrix for zeros:
      Z=len(zeros); L=2*n-1; J=np.zeros((Z,L)); diag=n-1
      for jj in range(Z):
          j=int(zeros[jj])
          J[jj,j:j+n] += om[j:j+n]? Wait Δj = + om[j:j+n] - h[diag-j:diag-j+n] -1 at diag. om is 1-h[::-1], length n. For positive part, +om. We can use slice of om: om[j:j+n]. Yes.
          J[jj, diag-j: diag+n?] diag-j length? diag-j + n <=? diag-j + n = 2n-2-j? Last index 2n-3-j? Actually previous: st=diag-j, d[st:st+n] += h for -? For j 0->1, d[st:st+n] -= h. So J[jj, st:st+n] -= h.
          J[jj, diag] -= 1.0
      # For each one i:
      best_move=None; best_score=score
      for ii in range(len(ones)):
          i=int(ones[ii])
          base = c.copy()
          base[i:i+n] -= om[i:i+n]
          st=diag-i
          base[st:st+n] += h[i? h[?]]? For i 1->0, += h. Which h slice? Previous: d[st:st+n] += h. h is old full array length n. Use h. But note boundaries? st:st+n length n. Good.
          base[diag] -= 1
          # candidate approx = base[None,:] + J
          approx = np.max(base[None,:] + J, axis=1)
          # Sort indices by approx; take top min(T, Z)
          # But need exact with cross corrections. Use top candidates maybe if any approx < m - 0.75 or approx near m.
          # Select all approx < best_score? best_score includes secondary. For simplicity top T by approx.
          if Z <= top_per_i: idx = np.arange(Z)
          else: idx = np.argpartition(approx, top_per_i-1)[:top_per_i]
          for jj in idx:
              j=int(zeros[jj])
              c2,b? = binary_trial(c,h,om,i,j,n)
              s2=score_c(c2)
              if s2 < best_score - 1e-9:
                  best_score=s2; best_move=(i,j,c2)
          # If best_move better than pass_best, store.
      if best_move is not None:
          i,j,c2=best_move
          # apply to h,om; recompute om? update
          c=c2; h[i]=0; h[j]=1; om[diag-i]=1; om[diag-j]=0
          score=best_score; moved=True
      if not moved: break
   return h, score, c

Detailed coordinate descent with score:
```
def score_c(c, win=0.25, coeff=0.01):
    m = float(c.max())
    if c.size == 0: return m
    pen = np.sum(np.maximum(0.0, c - (m - win)))
    return m + coeff * pen
```

Implement binary_scan:
```
def binary_scan(h,n,deadline,max_passes=20,top=40):
  h=project(np.where(h>0.5,1.,0.),n)
  c=corr; m=c.max(); om=1-h[::-1]; score=score_c(c)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_score=score; best_m=m
  passes=0
  while time<deadline and passes<max_passes:
    ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=...
    if len(ones)==0 ...
    Z=len(zeros); L=2*n-1; diag=n-1
    J=np.zeros((Z,L), dtype=float)
    for idx,j in enumerate(zeros):
       J[idx, j:j+n] += om[j:j+n]
       J[idx, diag-j: diag-j+n] -= h
       J[idx, diag] -= 1.0
    pass_best=None; pass_score=score
    for i in ones:
       base=c.copy()
       base[i:i+n] -= om[i:i+n]
       base[diag-i:diag-i+n] += h
       base[diag] -= 1.0
       # approximate max
       # To reduce memory, use np.maximum.reduce? cand = J + base[None,:]
       app = np.max(J + base[None,:], axis=1)
       # indices with app < m - 0.5? add; plus top top
       if Z<=top: idxs=np.arange(Z)
       else:
          # get top top by app and all with app < m-0.5
          idx_sort = np.argpartition(app, min(top,Z-1))[:min(top,Z-1)]
          idx_low = np.nonzero(app < m - 0.5)[0]
          idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx_sort, idx_low)))
       for jj in idxs:
          j=zeros[jj]
          c2=... binary_trial_no_copy? We can create from c each time.

Let's verify binary_trial formula one more time with continuous coordinate update to avoid bugs.
For single coordinate change from old to new (delta d), c_new = c_old + d*A - d^2 at diag, where A[p:p+n] += om (1 - h_rev) and A[diag-p:diag-p+n] -= h. For d=+1 (zero->one) at j: Δ = +om[j:j+n] - h[diag-j:diag-j+n] -1 at diag. For d=-1 at i: Δ = -om[i:i+n] + h[diag-i:diag-i+n] -1 at diag. Combine with cross corrections? The linear formula uses old om/h at i and j? For i and j changes, if segment overlaps, using old for both misses d_i*d_j terms. Self terms: both diagonal -1 each (since d_i^2=1,d_j^2=1) total -2. Cross terms: lag where i appears first and j second etc. Previous adds +1 at diag+i-j and diag+j-i. Let's trust.
But in binary_trial, after doing d[i:i+n] -= om; d[st_i:st_i+n] += h; d[j:j+n] += om; d[st_j:st_j+n] -= h; d[n-1]-=2; d[n-1+i-j]+=1; d[n-1+j-i]+=1. If i and j such that intervals overlap, corrections maybe need more than +1? Suppose i and j close, the omitted cross product d_i*d_j appears in c at multiple lags? Let's derive differential: c = bilinear B(h,h')+? Let change vector e = -e_i + e_j. Quadratic term = e^T H e. Linear terms use old. The cross term e_i*e_j could affect c at lags representing pair (i,j) only once? Since each pair of changed coordinates appears in exactly one correlation lag (their difference), there should be one or two corrections? For ordered full correlation, pair (i,j) contributes to c at lag j-i if h_i changes as first and h_j as second, and also if h_i second? The product h_a(1-h_b) is not symmetric, but for two changed coordinates, when a=i and b=j, and a=j,b=i, two lags (i-j and j-i), each cross term maybe +? Previous adds +1 each. Self diagonal two. Seems right.

If i and j such that j-i=0 impossible. If indices out of range? `diag+i-j` could be <0? i-j min -(n-1), diag - (n-1)=0; max diag+(n-1)=2n-2. Good.

After a move, update om: om = 1 - h[::-1]. If h[i] becomes0, om[diag-i] =1. If h[j]=1, om[diag-j]=0. Good.

def coord_cd(h,n,deadline,...):

    improved=False
    xs=np.linspace(0,1,grid)

      A.fill(0); A[p:p+n]+=om; A[diag-p:diag-p+n]-=x
      base_m=m? Actually c.max may update after accepted coords. Use cur_m=c.max()

      ii=int(np.argmin(vals))

      mm=coord_eval_max(c,A,xx,old,n); jj=argmin; bx=xx[jj]; bv=mm[jj]

    if not improved: break
  return best, compute_c5(best)
```

def coord_eval_max(c,A,xs,old,n):
  d=xs-old
  cand=c[None,:]+d[:,None]*A[None,:]
  cand[n-1] -= d*d  # this modifies first row? Need be careful: cand[:, n-1] -= d*d correct.
  return cand.max(axis=1)

def binary_stochastic(h,n,rng,deadline,initial_c=None):
  h=binarize(h,n); c=corr; om; score=score_c(c)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_score=score; best_c=c.copy(); best_m=c.max()
  no=0
  while time<deadline:
    # sample i/j with retries
    i=rng.randint(n); while h[i]<0.5: i=rng.randint(n)
    j=rng.randint(n); while h[j]>0.5: j=rng.randint(n)
    if i==j continue
    c2=binary_trial(c,h,om,i,j,n)
    m2=c2.max()
    m=c.max()? we maintain cur_m
    if m2 < cur_m -1e-6 or (abs(m2-cur_m)<1e-6 and score_c(c2)<score-1e-9):
       accept; no=0
       if score2<best_score: best update
    else:
       no +=1
    if no>2000: break
  return best_h, compute_c5(best_h)
```

Perturbation:
```
def perturb_binary(h,n,rng,k):
  h=h.copy()
  for _ in range(k):
    i=rng.randint(n) while h[i]<0.5
    j=rng.randint(n) while h[j]>0.5
    h[i]=0; h[j]=1
  return h
```
Each time compute c. ILS loop can use increasing k [1,2,3,5,8,16] to jump.

Let's refine `project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(h,n):
  h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
  t=0.5*n
  diff=t-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<=1e-12: return h
  # bisection shift
  lo=-1; hi=1
  for _ in range(60):
     m=0.5*(lo+hi); y=np.clip(h+m,0,1)
     if y.sum() < t: lo=m
     else: hi=m
  y=np.clip(h+hi,0,1)
  # adjust leftover on nonboundary
  d=t-y.sum(); mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
  if mask.any(): y[mask]+=d/mask.sum()
  else: idx=int(np.argmax(1-y)) if d>0 else int(np.argmax(y)); y[idx]=np.clip(y[idx]+d,0,1)
  # final exact correction
  d=t-y.sum(); if abs(d)>1e-15:
     mask=(y>1e-13)&(y<1-1e-13)
     if mask.any(): y[mask]+=d/mask.sum()
     else: ...
  return np.clip(y,0,1)
```
This modifies one/two entries; sum should be exact within float. Good.

`slsqp_polish(h,n,alphas,maxiter,deadline)`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,alphas,maxiter,deadline):
  best=project(h.copy(),n); bc=compute_c5(best,n)
  bounds=[(0,1)]*n
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
  for a in alphas:
    if time>deadline: break
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj,best,args=(n,a),jac=smooth_grad,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
    except Exception: continue
    nh=project(res.x,n); nc=compute_c5(nh,n)
    if nc<bc: best=nh; bc=nc
  return best,bc
```
Could be slow for 500 and maxiter 100. Use maxiter=50 for high alpha. alphas [500,5000,50000].

Smooth gradient uses `np.convolve`; for n=500 and alpha large, weights concentrated at max lags. Good.

`coord_cd` may be enough after SLSQP.

Now, potential issue: The objective is max over full correlation. Our coordinate update `A[p:p+n] += om; A[diag-p:diag-p+n] -= x` where `om` and `x` are current arrays. For p near edges, slices length n within L=2n-1: p+n <=2n-1? p max n-1 => p+n=2n-1, okay 1-based stop. diag-p = n-1-p >=0, +n <=2n-1? p=0 => n-1+n=2n-1, okay. Good.

Candidate c2 = c + d*A; c2[diag]-=d*d. This is length L. Good.

Coordinate score: We update `c` and `cur_m` after accept, and `om`. For next coordinate, `c` updated, `om` updated, `x` updated. Good.

```
def greedy_h(n, rng, target_score=True):
  N=n//2
  h=np.zeros(n,float); cp=np.zeros(n,float); cn=np.zeros(n,float); ones=0
  for p in range(n):
    rem=n-p
    prev=h[:p][::-1] if p>0 else np.array([])
    best_b=None; best_score=1e100; best_cp=None; best_cn=None
    for b in (0.0,1.0):
      no=ones+b
      if no>N or no+rem-1<N: continue
      if b>0.5:
        add=1.0-prev
        cp2=cp + add
        cn2=cn
      else:
        add=prev
        cp2=cp
        cn2=cn + add
      m1=cp2[:p+1].max() if p>0 else 0.0
      m2=cn2[:p+1].max() if p>0 else 0.0
      m=max(m1,m2)
      # partial count at active
      acts = int(np.count_nonzero(cp2[:p+1] >= m-0.5)) + int(np.count_nonzero(cn2[:p+1] >= m-0.5))
      bias = 0.05 * abs(no - N*p/n)
      s = m + 0.01*acts + bias + 1e-6*rng.random()
      if s < best_score:
         best_score=s; best_b=b; best_cp=cp2; best_cn=cn2; best_no=no
    if best_b is None: best_b=1.0 if ones<N else 0.0; best_cp=cp; best_cn=cn; best_no=ones
    h[p]=best_b; cp=best_cp; cn=best_cn; ones=int(best_no)
  return h
```
Note `cp2=cp + add` creates arrays length n, but only first p used. For p=0, prev empty; cp2=cp if b0; m=0. Bias pushes no close to N*p/n. This might help.

def beam_h(n,width,deadline):

   cand=[]

      for b in (0,1):

        if no>N or no+rem<N: continue

        h2=hst.copy(); h2[p]=b
        m=max(cp2[:p+1].max(initial=0), cn2[:p+1].max(initial=0))
        acts=... 
        bias=0.05*abs(no-N*(p+1)/n)

   if not cand: break
   if len(cand)>width:

 for state in states:
   h=state[0].astype(float); c5=compute_c5(h,n)
   if best is None or c5<best: best=c5; bh=h
 return bh,best

```
def score_c(c, win=0.5, coeff=0.01):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if c.size==0: return m
    pen=float(np.sum(np.maximum(0.0, c-(m-win))))
    return m + coeff*pen
```
For continuous coordinate maybe call with win=0.2? We'll have parameter default. In coord, use `score_c(c,0.2,0.01)`. In binary, `score_c(c,0.5,0.02)`? Let's define separate functions:

```

`coord_cd`: Need initial best c5 maybe `c5(best,n)`. But we track score. Return best, c5. Use deadline check. In candidate selection, `coord_eval_max` allocate. Use `grid` maybe 32. For n>600, use grid 24 to save. Use refine 12.
Pseudo:
```
def coord_cd(h,n,deadline,max_passes=4,grid=32,refine=12):
  best=project_simplex(h.copy(),n)
  bc=float(c5(best,n))
  best_score=score_cont(corr_vec(best,n))
  L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; A=np.zeros(L)
  xs=np.linspace(0,1,grid)
  for _ in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    x=best.copy(); c=corr_vec(x,n); cur_m=float(c.max()); scur=score_cont(c)
    om=1-x[::-1]; improved=False
    for p in range(n):
      old=x[p]
      A.fill(0); A[p:p+n]+=om; A[diag-p:diag-p+n]-=x
      vals=coord_eval_max(c,A,xs,old,n)
      ii=int(np.argmin(vals))
      sp=1.0/(grid-1)
      lo=max(0.0,xs[ii]-sp); hi=min(1.0,xs[ii]+sp)
      xx=np.linspace(lo,hi,refine+1)
      mm=coord_eval_max(c,A,xx,old,n)
      jj=int(np.argmin(mm)); bx=float(xx[jj])
      if lo<bx<hi:
         sp2=(hi-lo)/refine; lo2=max(0,bx-sp2); hi2=min(1,bx+sp2)
         yy=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,9)
         ww=coord_eval_max(c,A,yy,old,n)
         kk=int(np.argmin(ww))
         if ww[kk] <= mm[jj]: bx=float(yy[kk])
      d=bx-old
      if abs(d)<1e-12: continue
      c2=c + d*A; c2[diag]-=d*d
      m2=float(c2.max())
      if m2 < cur_m - 1e-10:
         improved=True; c=c2; x[p]=bx; om[diag-p]=1-bx; cur_m=m2; scur=score_cont(c)
      elif abs(m2-cur_m)<=1e-8:
         s2=score_cont(c2)
         if s2 < scur - 1e-10:
            improved=True; c=c2; x[p]=bx; om[diag-p]=1-bx; scur=s2
    if not improved:
       break
    xp=project_simplex(x,n); cp=corr_vec(xp); sp=score_cont(cp); c5p=float(cp.max())*2/n
    if sp < best_score -1e-12:
       best=xp; best_score=sp; bc=c5p
  return best, bc
```

def binarize(h,n):
  if n%2: return None
  target=n//2
  if target<=0 or target>=n: return zeros? but sum not n/2 if target=n? not.

`binary_trial`: Ensure c2 is copy. Use dtype float.
```
def binary_trial(c,h,om,i,j,n):
  d=c.copy()
  d[i:i+n] -= om
  st=n-1-i
  d[st:st+n] += h
  d[j:j+n] += om
  st=n-1-j
  d[st:st+n] -= h
  d[n-1]-=2.0
  d[n-1+i-j]+=1.0
  d[n-1+j-i]+=1.0
  return d
```
But `om` length n; d slice i:i+n okay. `h` slice st:st+n okay. It uses h,om old. Good.

`binary_stochastic`: Need current `cur_m`. Recompute score? We can maintain. Use `score_binary`.
```
def binary_stochastic(h,n,rng,deadline):
  b=binarize(h,n)
  if b is None: return None, inf
  c=corr_vec(b,n); om=1-b[::-1]
  cur_m=float(c.max()); score=score_binary(c)
  best=b.copy(); best_c=c5(b,n); best_score=score
  no=0
  while time.time()<deadline:
    # sample
    for _ in range(10):
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); if b[i]>0.5: break
    for _ in range(10):
       j=int(rng.randint(n)); if b[j]<0.5: break
    if i==j: no+=1; continue
    c2=binary_trial(c, b, om, i,j,n)
    m2=float(c2.max())
    if m2 < cur_m -1e-6 or (abs(m2-cur_m)<1e-6):
       s2=score_binary(c2)
       if s2 < score -1e-9:
          c=c2; b[i]=0; b[j]=1; om[n-1-i]=1; om[n-1-j]=0
          cur_m=m2; score=s2; no=0
          if s2 < best_score:
             best=b.copy(); best_score=s2; best_c=c5(b,n)
          continue
       # occasional tie exploration
       if abs(s2-score)<=1e-9 and rng.random()<0.05:
          c=c2; b[i]=0; b[j]=1; om...; cur_m=m2; score=s2; no+=1; continue
    no+=1
    if no>20000: break
  return best, best_c
```

def binary_optimize(h_init,n,rng,deadline):
  b=binarize(h_init,n)
  if b is None: return None,inf,inf
  best_h=b; best_c=c5(b,n); best_score=score_binary(corr_vec(b,n))? maybe compute
  phase=0; no_improve=0
  ks=[0,1,2,3,5,8,15,30]
  while time<deadline and no_improve<4:
    # stochastic chunk
    t=min(time+10,deadline-20)
    h1,c1=binary_stochastic(b,n,rng,t)
    if h1 is not None:
       s1=score_binary(corr_vec(h1,n))
       if c1 < best_c -1e-12 or (abs(c1-best_c)<=1e-12 and s1<best_score-1e-9):
          best_h=h1; best_c=c1; best_score=s1; no_improve=0
       b=h1
    # scan chunk
    t=min(time+20,deadline-10)
    h2,c2,s2=binary_scan(b,n,t,max_passes=8, top=80 if n<=400 else 50)
    if c2 < best_c -1e-12 or (abs(c2-best_c)<=1e-12 and s2<best_score-1e-9):
       best_h=h2; best_c=c2; best_score=s2; no_improve=0
       b=h2
    else:
       no_improve+=1
    k=ks[phase % len(ks)]; phase+=1
    if k==0:
       b=best_h.copy()
    else:
       bpy=best_h.copy()
       for _ in range(k):
          # swap
          i=j=-1
          for _2 in range(20):
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); if bpy[i]>0.5: break
          for _2 in range(20):
             j=int(rng.randint(n)); if bpy[j]<0.5: break
          if i!=j:
             bpy[i]=0; bpy[j]=1
       b=bpy
  return best_h,best_c
```

`h_from_kind(n,kind,seed,rng)`:
```
def h_from_kind(n, kind, seed, rng):
  i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
  if kind==0:
    h=np.sin(np.pi*i/n)**2
  elif kind==1:
    t = i / max(1,n-1)
    p=1+int(seed%3)
    f=t**p - np.mean(t**p)
  elif kind==2:
    freq=1+int((seed%7))
    phase=0.5*math.pi*int((seed//7)%4)
    f=np.cos(2*math.pi*freq*i/n + phase) - np.mean(...)
  elif kind==3:
    # random sum 3 waves
    f=np.zeros(n)
    for q in range(1,4):
       a=rng.normal(); ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
       f += a*np.cos(2*math.pi*q*i/n + ph)
  else: f=np.zeros(n)
  if kind !=0:
    f=f-np.mean(f)
    sc=np.max(np.abs(f))
    if sc>1e-12:
       amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.0) if rng else 1.0
       f=f/sc*amp
    h=0.5+0.5*f
  return project_simplex(np.clip(h,0,1),n)
```

Detailed functions:

```
def proj(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).clip(0,1)
    t=0.5*n
    if abs(h.sum()-t) < 1e-13: return h
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       m=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if np.clip(h+m,0,1).sum() < t: lo=m
       else: hi=m
    y=np.clip(h+hi,0,1)
    d=t-y.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-12:
       mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): y[mask]+=d/mask.sum()
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-y)) if d>0 else int(np.argmax(y))
          y[idx]=float(np.clip(y[idx]+d,0,1))
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    d=t-y.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-14:
       mask=(y>1e-13)&(y<1-1e-13)
       if mask.any(): y[mask]+=d/mask.sum()
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-y)) if d>0 else int(np.argmax(y))
          y[idx]=float(np.clip(y[idx]+d,0,1))
    return y
```
If d large and mask none, one idx may exceed by >1? But if all at bounds and sum not t, impossible? e.g., h all0,n>0 target n/2, lo/hi bisection should find y partial bounds, mask likely. Good.

`corr_vec`:
```
def corr_vec(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode="full")
```

`c5`:
```
def c5(h,n): return float(np.max(corr_vec(h,n)))* (2.0/n)
```

`score_base`:
```
def score_base(c, win, coeff):
   m=float(np.max(c)); pen=float(np.sum(np.maximum(0.0,c-(m-win)))); return m+coeff*pen
def score_binary(c): return score_base(c,0.5,0.02)
def score_cont(c): return score_base(c,0.08,0.01)
```

`smooth_val_grad`:
```
def smooth_val_grad(x,n,alpha):
   c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
   m=float(c.max())
   r=alpha*(c-m)
   r-=r.max()
   ee=np.exp(r)
   z=float(ee.sum())
    mm=m+math.log(z)/alpha
   dx=2.0/n
   w=ee/z
   A=np.convolve(1.0-x,w,'full')
   B=np.convolve(x,w[::-1],'full')
   s=n-1
   return mm*dx, (A-B)[s:s+n]*dx
```
If alpha huge, r after subtract max ≤0, fine. `math.log(z)` z maybe 0? no.

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,alphas,maxiter,deadline):
  best=proj(h.copy(),n); bc=float(c5(best,n))
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  cons=[{"type":"eq","fun":sum_con,"jac":sum_con_jac,"args":(n,)}]
  for a in alphas:
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    try:
      res=minimize(smooth_obj,best,args=(n,a),jac=smooth_grad,method="SLSQP",bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={"maxiter":int(maxiter),"ftol":1e-12})
    except Exception:
      continue
    if not hasattr(res,"x") or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): continue
    nh=proj(res.x,n); nc=float(c5(nh,n))
    if nc<bc:
      best=nh; bc=nc
  return best,bc
```

`sum_con`, `sum_con_jac`.

`coord_eval_max`:
```
def coord_eval_max(c,A,xs,old,n):
   d=xs-old
   cand=c[None,:]+d[:,None]*A[None,:]
   cand[:,n-1]-=d*d
   return cand.max(axis=1)
```

`coord_cd` as above. Need if grid-1 zero? set grid>=2. Use `grid = max(2, int(grid))`.

In coord_cd candidate refine: if `lo` and `hi` same? Maybe. Use guard.

```
def coord_cd(h,n,deadline,max_passes=4,grid=32,refine=12):
    best=proj(h.copy(),n)
    bc=float(c5(best,n))
    best_score=score_cont(corr_vec(best,n))
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; A=np.zeros(L)
    grid=max(3,int(grid)); refine=max(3,int(refine))
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,grid)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       x=best.copy(); c=corr_vec(x,n); cur_m=float(c.max()); sc=score_cont(c)
       om=1.0-x[::-1]; improved=False
       for p in range(n):
          old=float(x[p])
          A.fill(0.0)
          A[p:p+n]+=om
          A[diag-p:diag-p+n]-=x
          vals=coord_eval_max(c,A,xs,old,n)
          ii=int(np.argmin(vals))
          sp=1.0/(grid-1)
          lo=max(0.0,float(xs[ii])-sp); hi=min(1.0,float(xs[ii])+sp)
          xx=np.linspace(lo,hi,refine+1)
          mm=coord_eval_max(c,A,xx,old,n)
          jj=int(np.argmin(mm)); bx=float(xx[jj])
          if lo < bx < hi:
             sp2=(hi-lo)/float(refine)
             lo2=max(0.0,bx-sp2); hi2=min(1.0,bx+sp2)
             yy=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,9)
             ww=coord_eval_max(c,A,yy,old,n)
             kk=int(np.argmin(ww))
             if ww[kk] <= mm[jj]: bx=float(yy[kk])
          d=bx-old
          if abs(d) < 1e-12: continue
          c2=c + d*A
          c2[diag]-=d*d
          m2=float(c2.max())
          take=False; s2=0.0
          if m2 < cur_m - 1e-10:
             take=True; s2=score_cont(c2)
          elif abs(m2-cur_m) <= 1e-8:
             s2=score_cont(c2)
             if s2 < sc - 1e-10: take=True
          if take:
             c=c2; x[p]=bx; om[diag-p]=1.0-bx; cur_m=m2; sc=s2; improved=True
       if not improved: break
       xp=proj(x,n); cp=corr_vec(xp); sp=score_cont(cp); c5p=float(cp.max())*2.0/n
       if sp < best_score -1e-12:
          best=xp; best_score=sp; bc=c5p
    return best, float(c5(best,n))
```
If `best` not updated due raw same score lower? We update if sp lower. Good. `bc` may stale if sp lower with same raw? c5p same, but okay. Return compute final.

`binary_stochastic`: Need ensure if `rng.random` not available? Use rng.rand(). `np.random.RandomState` has random_sample? use `rng.rand()`.
```
def binary_stochastic(h,n,rng,deadline):
    b=binarize(h,n)
    if b is None: return None, float("inf")
    c=corr_vec(b,n); om=1.0-b[::-1]
    cur_m=float(c.max()); score=score_binary(c)
    best=b.copy(); best_c=float(c5(b,n)); best_score=score
    no=0
    diag=n-1
    while time.time()<deadline:
       i=-1
       for _ in range(12):
          i=int(rng.randint(n))
          if b[i] > 0.5: break
       j=-1
       for _ in range(12):
          j=int(rng.randint(n))
          if b[j] < 0.5: break
       if i<0 or j<0 or i==j:
          no+=1
       else:
          c2=binary_trial(c,b,om,i,j,n)
          m2=float(c2.max())
          if m2 < cur_m - 1e-6 or abs(m2-cur_m) < 1e-6:
             s2=score_binary(c2)
             if s2 < score - 1e-9:
                c=c2; b[i]=0.0; b[j]=1.0; om[diag-i]=1.0; om[diag-j]=0.0
                cur_m=m2; score=s2; no=0
                if s2 < best_score:
                   best=b.copy(); best_score=s2; best_c=float(c5(b,n))
                continue
             if abs(s2-score) <= 1e-9 and rng.rand() < 0.05:
                c=c2; ...; cur_m=m2; score=s2; no+=1; continue
       no += 1
       if no > 30000: break
    return best, best_c
```
If tie exploration changes score same? It may wander; best_score unchanged. Good.

`binary_scan`:
```
def binary_scan(h,n,deadline,max_passes=10,top=60):
    b=binarize(h,n)
    if b is None: return None, float("inf"), float("inf")
    c=corr_vec(b,n); om=1-b[::-1]
    cur_m=float(c.max()); score=score_binary(c)
    best=b.copy(); best_m=cur_m; best_score=score
    diag=n-1; L=2*n-1
    passes=0
    use_scan = (n <= 600)
    while time.time()<deadline and passes<max_passes and use_scan:
       ones=np.nonzero(b>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(b<0.5)[0]
       if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
       Z=zeroes.size; topk=min(int(top),Z)
       J=np.zeros((Z,L), dtype=float)
       for zi in range(Z):
           j=int(zeros[zi])
           J[zi,j:j+n]+=om[j:j+n]
           J[zi,diag-j:diag-j+n]-=b
           J[zi,diag]-=1.0
       pass_best=None; p_score=score
       for ii in range(ones.size):
           i=int(ones[ii])
           base=c.copy()
           base[i:i+n]-=om[i:i+n]
           base[diag-i:diag-i+n]+=b
           base[diag]-=1.0
           if Z <= topk:
              idxs=np.arange(Z)
           else:
              app=np.max(J + base[None,:], axis=1)
              idxs=np.argpartition(app, topk-1)[:topk]
              idx_low=np.nonzero(app < cur_m - 0.6)[0]
              if idx_low.size:
                 idxs=np.unique(np.concatenate((idxs, idx_low)))
           for jj in idxs:
              j=int(zeros[jj])
              c2=binary_trial(c,b,om,i,j,n)
              m2=float(c2.max())
              if m2 > cur_m + 1e-6: continue
              s2=score_binary(c2)
              if s2 < p_score - 1e-9:
                  pass_best=(i,j,c2,m2,s2); p_score=s2
       if pass_best is None: break
       i,j,c2,m2,s2=pass_best
       b[i]=0.0; b[j]=1.0; c=c2; om[diag-i]=1.0; om[diag-j]=0.0
       cur_m=m2; score=s2; passes+=1
       if s2 < best_score -1e-12:
           best=b.copy(); best_score=s2; best_m=m2
    return best, (best_m*2.0/n), best_score
```
Important: `p_score` initialized to current score at start of pass. If a move lowers score but raw same, accepted. If another move in same pass could build on it, we don't; next pass. Good.
Potential issue: `J + base[None,:]` creates array with shape (Z,L). If Z=0 skip. Good.
`np.argpartition(app, topk-1)` if topk=Z? handled.
If `idx_low` includes many, `np.unique` could be large. If cur_m low and many app<cur_m-0.6, could exact many. But that's okay? Could be all 250 with m drop potential; exact 250 per i worst 62k exact, heavy. Limit `idx_low` to topk? We can combine by taking first topk of low sorted? Simpler: if idx_low.size > topk, `idx_low = idx_low[np.argpartition(app[idx_low], topk-1)[:topk]]`. Let's add to avoid explosion.
```
if idx_low.size:
   if idx_low.size > topk: idx_low=idx_low[np.argpartition(app[idx_low], topk-1)[:topk]]
   idxs=np.unique(np.concatenate(...))
```
Good.

`binary_optimize`:
```
def binary_optimize(h_init,n,rng,deadline):
    b=binarize(h_init,n)
    if b is None: return None,float("inf"),float("inf")
    c=corr_vec(b,n); best=b.copy(); best_c=float(cmax*2/n); best_score=score_binary(c)
    phase=0; stalls=0
    ks=(0,1,2,3,5,8,15,30,60,120)
    while time.time()<deadline and stalls < 5:
       t=min(time.time()+10.0, deadline-20.0)
       h1,c1=binary_stochastic(b,n,rng,t)
       if h1 is not None:
           s1=score_binary(corr_vec(h1,n))
           if c1 < best_c -1e-12 or (abs(c1-best_c)<=1e-12 and s1 < best_score -1e-9):
              best=h1; best_c=c1; best_score=s1; stalls=0
           b=h1
       t=min(time.time()+25.0, deadline-10.0)
       top = 70 if n <= 450 else 45
       h2,c2,s2=binary_scan(b,n,t,max_passes=8,top=top)
       if h2 is not None:
           if c2 < best_c -1e-12 or (abs(c2-best_c)<=1e-12 and s2 < best_score -1e-9):
              best=h2; best_c=c2; best_score=s2; stalls=0
           b=h2
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       k=ks[phase % len(ks)]
       phase += 1
       b=best.copy()
       if k:
          diag=n-1 # not needed
          for _ in range(k):
             i=-1; j=-1
             for _ in range(20):
                ii=int(rng.randint(n))
                if b[ii]>0.5: i=ii; break
             for _ in range(20):
                jj=int(rng.randint(n))
                if b[jj]<0.5: j=jj; break
             if i>=0 and j>=0 and i!=j:
                b[i]=0.0; b[j]=1.0
          # if k large, b still count exact? swaps equal.
    return best,best_c,best_score
```
Issue: If stochastic or scan improves `best` but raw same score lower, we update best_score. After phase, perturbation from best. If no raw lower but score lower, stalls reset? We reset only if best improved (score). Good. `stalls` increments only if no improvement? Actually if c same and score no lower, stalls not incremented. Need increment after phase if no best improvement. We can track `improved_phase=False`; set when best update. At end if not improved_phase: stalls+=1. Use.
```
improved=False
... if update best: improved=True
...
if not improved: stalls +=1
```
Good.

`transfer_h`:
```
def transfer_h(h,n,n2):
   if n2==n: return proj(h.copy(),n).copy()
   x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n); y=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n2)
   return proj(np.interp(y,x,h),n2)
```

`greedy_h` and `beam_h` as above. In greedy, using `cp2=cp + add` where add length p; need align to first p? cp is length n; adding add to full n would add to indices beyond p too if add shape p. Need add array length n with nonzero first p? In pair additions for lag d=1..p, cp[d] changes. `prev` corresponds to d=1..p? prev = h[:p][::-1] gives indices p-1,p-2,...,0; for d=1 prev h[p-1], d=p h[0]. We need add to cp[1:p+1], not cp[0:p]. If we do cp + prev, shape mismatch; if append zero at index0? Let's construct `addp` length n zeros, `addp[1:p+1] = ...`. In greedy for speed, create `cp2=cp.copy(); if p>0: cp2[1:p+1]+=...`. Similarly cn. That avoids length issues. For beam too.

Greedy implementation:
```
for p:
  best...
  for b:
    cp2=cp.copy(); cn2=cn.copy()

       prev=h[:p][::-1]

       else: cp2[1:p+1] += prev
    m = max(cp2[:p+1].max(), cn2[:p+1].max())
```

Now, run detailed:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); budget=float(budget_s); deadline=t0+max(5.0,budget-5.0)
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try:
     h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=int(h0.size)
  except Exception:
     n0=500; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
  h0=proj(h0,n0)
  best_h=h0.copy(); best_n=n0; best_c=float(c5(h0,n0))
  # sym
  for tr in (0,1,2):
     if tr==1: v=0.5*(h0+h0[::-1])
     elif tr==2: v=0.5*(h0+(1.0-h0[::-1]))
     else: v=h0
     v=proj(v,n0); cv=c5(v,n0)
     if cv<best_c: best_h=v; best_c=cv
  # candidate sweep at initial n and maybe some n

```
def h_candidate(n, kind, sd, rng=None):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   if kind==0:
      h=np.sin(np.pi*i/float(n))**2
      return proj(h,n)
   if kind==1:
      t=i/max(1.0,float(n-1)); p=1+(sd%3)
      f=t**float(p)-np.mean(t**float(p))
   elif kind==2:
      q=1+(sd%5); ph=0.5*math.pi*((sd//5)%4)
      f=np.cos(2.0*math.pi*float(q)*i/float(n)+ph); f-=np.mean(f)
   else:
      if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
      f=np.zeros(n)
      for q in range(1,4):
         f += rng.normal()*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*float(q)*i/float(n)+rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi))
   f=f-np.mean(f); sc=np.max(np.abs(f))
   if sc>1e-14:
      amp=0.5+0.5*(sd%3)/2.0? maybe deterministic; or rng.uniform. Use amp=0.8 if rng none.
      f=f/sc*amp
   h=0.5+0.5*f
   return proj(h,n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def proj(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).clip(0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * n
    if abs(h.sum() - t) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(h + m, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    y = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - y.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-12:
        mask = (y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            y[mask] += d / mask.sum()
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmax(y))
            y[idx] = float(np.clip(y[idx] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - y.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-14:
        mask = (y > 1e-13) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-13)
        if mask.any():
            y[mask] += d / mask.sum()
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmax(y))
            y[idx] = float(np.clip(y[idx] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
    return y


def corr_vec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_of(h, n):
    return float(np.max(corr_vec(h, n))) * (2.0 / n)


def pen_bin(c):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    return float(np.sum(np.maximum(0.0, c - (m - 0.5))))


def pen_cont(c):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    return float(np.sum(np.maximum(0.0, c - (m - 0.1))))


def lex_better(m, p, bm, bp, eps=1e-8):
    return (m < bm - eps) or (abs(m - bm) <= eps and p < bp - eps)


def sum_con(x, n):
    return float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n


def sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    r = alpha * (c - m)
    r -= r.max()
    ee = np.exp(r)
    z = float(ee.sum())
    val = (m + math.log(z) / alpha) * (2.0 / n)
    w = ee / z
    A = np.convolve(1.0 - x, w, mode="full")
    B = np.convolve(x, w[::-1], mode="full")
    s = n - 1
    return val, (A - B)[s:s + n] * (2.0 / n)


def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha)[0]


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha)[1]


def slsqp_polish(h, n, alphas, maxiter, deadline):
    best = proj(h.copy(), n)
    bc = float(c5_of(best, n))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_con, "jac": sum_con_jac, "args": (n,)}]
    for a in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj, best, args=(n, a), jac=smooth_grad,
                           method="SLSQP", bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                           options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12})
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not hasattr(res, "x") or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            continue
        nh = proj(res.x, n)
        nc = float(c5_of(nh, n))
        if nc < bc:
            best = nh
            bc = nc
    return best, bc


def coord_eval_max(c, A, xs, old, n):
    d = xs - old
    cand = c[None, :] + d[:, None] * A[None, :]
    cand[:, n - 1] -= d * d
    return cand.max(axis=1)


def coord_cd(h, n, deadline, max_passes=4, grid=32, refine=12):
    best = proj(h.copy(), n)
    c0 = corr_vec(best, n)
    best_m = float(np.max(c0))
    best_p = pen_cont(c0)
    best_c5 = best_m * (2.0 / n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    A = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    grid = max(3, int(grid))
    refine = max(3, int(refine))
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, grid)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        x = best.copy()
        c = corr_vec(x, n)
        cur_m = float(np.max(c))
        cur_p = pen_cont(c)
        om = 1.0 - x[::-1]
        improved = False
        for p in range(n):
            old = float(x[p])
            A.fill(0.0)
            A[p:p + n] += om
            A[diag - p:diag - p + n] -= x
            vals = coord_eval_max(c, A, xs, old, n)
            ii = int(np.argmin(vals))
            sp = 1.0 / (grid - 1)
            lo = max(0.0, float(xs[ii]) - sp)
            hi = min(1.0, float(xs[ii]) + sp)
            xx = np.linspace(lo, hi, refine + 1)
            mm = coord_eval_max(c, A, xx, old, n)
            jj = int(np.argmin(mm))
            bx = float(xx[jj])
            if lo < bx < hi:
                sp2 = (hi - lo) / float(refine)
                lo2 = max(0.0, bx - sp2)
                hi2 = min(1.0, bx + sp2)
                yy = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 9)
                ww = coord_eval_max(c, A, yy, old, n)
                kk = int(np.argmin(ww))
                if ww[kk] <= mm[jj]:
                    bx = float(yy[kk])
            d = bx - old
            if abs(d) < 1e-12:
                continue
            c2 = c + d * A
            c2[diag] -= d * d
            m2 = float(np.max(c2))
            take = False
            p2 = 0.0
            if m2 < cur_m - 1e-9:
                take = True
                p2 = pen_cont(c2)
            elif abs(m2 - cur_m) <= 1e-7:
                p2 = pen_cont(c2)
                if p2 < cur_p - 1e-9:
                    take = True
            if take:
                c = c2
                x[p] = bx
                om[diag - p] = 1.0 - bx
                cur_m = m2
                cur_p = p2
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        xp = proj(x, n)
        cp = corr_vec(xp, n)
        mp = float(np.max(cp))
        pp = pen_cont(cp)
        if lex_better(mp, pp, best_m, best_p):
            best = xp
            best_m = mp
            best_p = pp
            best_c5 = mp * (2.0 / n)
    return best, best_c5


def binarize(h, n):
    if n % 2:
        return None
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0 or target >= n:
        return None
    idx = np.argpartition(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), target - 1)[:target]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def binary_trial(c, h, om, i, j, n):
    d = c.copy()
    d[i:i + n] -= om
    st = n - 1 - i
    d[st:st + n] += h
    d[j:j + n] += om
    st = n - 1 - j
    d[st:st + n] -= h
    d[n - 1] -= 2.0
    d[n - 1 + i - j] += 1.0
    d[n - 1 + j - i] += 1.0
    return d


def binary_stochastic(h, n, rng, deadline):
    b = binarize(h, n)
    if b is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    c = corr_vec(b, n)
    om = 1.0 - b[::-1]
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    cur_p = pen_bin(c)
    best = b.copy()
    best_m = cur_m
    best_p = cur_p
    best_c5 = best_m * (2.0 / n)
    no = 0
    diag = n - 1
    while time.time() < deadline:
        i = -1
        for _ in range(12):
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            if b[i] > 0.5:
                break
        j = -1
        for _ in range(12):
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if b[j] < 0.5:
                break
        if i < 0 or j < 0 or i == j:
            no += 1
        else:
            c2 = binary_trial(c, b, om, i, j, n)
            m2 = float(np.max(c2))
            if m2 < cur_m - 1e-6 or abs(m2 - cur_m) < 1e-6:
                p2 = pen_bin(c2)
                if lex_better(m2, p2, cur_m, cur_p):
                    c = c2
                    b[i] = 0.0
                    b[j] = 1.0
                    om[diag - i] = 1.0
                    om[diag - j] = 0.0
                    cur_m = m2
                    cur_p = p2
                    no = 0
                    if lex_better(m2, p2, best_m, best_p):
                        best = b.copy()
                        best_m = m2
                        best_p = p2
                        best_c5 = m2 * (2.0 / n)
                    continue
                if abs(p2 - cur_p) <= 1e-9 and rng.rand() < 0.05:
                    c = c2
                    b[i] = 0.0
                    b[j] = 1.0
                    om[diag - i] = 1.0
                    om[diag - j] = 0.0
                    cur_m = m2
                    cur_p = p2
                    no += 1
                    continue
        no += 1
        if no > 30000:
            break
        if no % 8000 == 0:
            c = corr_vec(b, n)
            cur_m = float(np.max(c))
            cur_p = pen_bin(c)
    return best, best_c5


def binary_scan(h, n, deadline, max_passes=10, top=60):
    b = binarize(h, n)
    if b is None:
        return None, float("inf"), float("inf")
    c = corr_vec(b, n)
    om = 1.0 - b[::-1]
    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    cur_p = pen_bin(c)
    best = b.copy()
    best_m = cur_m
    best_p = cur_p
    diag = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    passes = 0
    use_scan = (n <= 650)
    while time.time() < deadline and passes < max_passes and use_scan:
        c = corr_vec(b, n)
        cur_m = float(np.max(c))
        cur_p = pen_bin(c)
        om = 1.0 - b[::-1]
        ones = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        Z = int(zeros.size)
        topk = min(int(top), Z)
        J = np.zeros((Z, L), dtype=float)
        for zi in range(Z):
            j = int(zeros[zi])
            J[zi, j:j + n] += om[j:j + n]
            J[zi, diag - j:diag - j + n] -= b
            J[zi, diag] -= 1.0
        pass_best = None
        pass_m = cur_m
        pass_p = cur_p
        for ii in range(ones.size):
            i = int(ones[ii])
            base = c.copy()
            base[i:i + n] -= om[i:i + n]
            base[diag - i:diag - i + n] += b
            base[diag] -= 1.0
            if Z <= topk:
                idxs = np.arange(Z)
            else:
                app = np.max(J + base[None, :], axis=1)
                idxs = np.argpartition(app, topk - 1)[:topk]
                idx_low = np.nonzero(app < cur_m - 0.6)[0]
                if idx_low.size:
                    if idx_low.size > topk:
                        idx_low = idx_low[np.argpartition(app[idx_low], topk - 1)[:topk]]
                    idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((idxs, idx_low)))
            for jj in idxs:
                j = int(zeros[jj])
                c2 = binary_trial(c, b, om, i, j, n)
                m2 = float(np.max(c2))
                if m2 > cur_m + 1e-6:
                    continue
                p2 = pen_bin(c2)
                if lex_better(m2, p2, pass_m, pass_p):
                    pass_best = (i, j, c2, m2, p2)
                    pass_m = m2
                    pass_p = p2
        if pass_best is None:
            break
        i, j, c2, m2, p2 = pass_best
        b[i] = 0.0
        b[j] = 1.0
        c = c2
        om[diag - i] = 1.0
        om[diag - j] = 0.0
        cur_m = m2
        cur_p = p2
        passes += 1
        if lex_better(m2, p2, best_m, best_p):
            best = b.copy()
            best_m = m2
            best_p = p2
    return best, best_m * (2.0 / n), best_p


def binary_optimize(h_init, n, rng, deadline):
    b = binarize(h_init, n)
    if b is None:
        return None, float("inf"), float("inf")
    c = corr_vec(b, n)
    best = b.copy()
    best_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_p = pen_bin(c)
    best_c5 = best_m * (2.0 / n)
    phase = 0
    stalls = 0
    ks = (0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 15, 30, 60, 120)
    while time.time() < deadline and stalls < 5:
        improved = False
        t1 = min(time.time() + 10.0, deadline - 20.0)
        h1, c1 = binary_stochastic(b, n, rng, t1)
        if h1 is not None:
            s1 = pen_bin(corr_vec(h1, n))
            if lex_better(float(c1 * n / 2.0), s1, best_m, best_p):
                best = h1
                best_m = float(c1 * n / 2.0)
                best_p = s1
                best_c5 = c1
                improved = True
            b = h1
        t2 = min(time.time() + 25.0, deadline - 10.0)
        top = 75 if n <= 450 else 45
        h2, c2, s2 = binary_scan(b, n, t2, max_passes=8, top=top)
        if h2 is not None:
            m2 = float(c2 * n / 2.0)
            if lex_better(m2, s2, best_m, best_p):
                best = h2
                best_m = m2
                best_p = s2
                best_c5 = c2
                improved = True
            b = h2
        if not improved:
            stalls += 1
        k = ks[phase % len(ks)]
        phase += 1
        b = best.copy()
        for _ in range(k):
            i = -1
            j = -1
            for _ in range(20):
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                if b[ii] > 0.5:
                    i = ii
                    break
            for _ in range(20):
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if b[jj] < 0.5:
                    j = jj
                    break
            if i >= 0 and j >= 0 and i != j:
                b[i] = 0.0
                b[j] = 1.0
    return best, best_c5, best_p


def transfer_h(h, n, n2):
    if n2 == n:
        return proj(h.copy(), n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2)
    return proj(np.interp(y, x, h), n2)


def greedy_h(n, rng):
    N = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    cp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    cn = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    ones = 0
    for p in range(n):
        rem = n - p
        prev = h[:p][::-1] if p > 0 else np.zeros(0)
        best_b = None
        best_s = 1e100
        best_cp = None
        best_cn = None
        best_no = 0
        for b in (0.0, 1.0):
            no = ones + b
            if no > N or no + rem - 1 < N:
                continue
            cp2 = cp.copy()
            cn2 = cn.copy()
            if p > 0:
                if b > 0.5:
                    cn2[1:p + 1] += 1.0 - prev
                else:
                    cp2[1:p + 1] += prev
            m1 = float(cp2[:p + 1].max())
            m2 = float(cn2[:p + 1].max())
            m = max(m1, m2)
            acts = int(np.count_nonzero(cp2[:p + 1] >= m - 0.5)) + int(
                np.count_nonzero(cn2[:p + 1] >= m - 0.5))
            bias = 0.05 * abs(no - N * float(p + 1) / float(n))
            s = m + 0.01 * float(acts) + bias + 1e-6 * rng.rand()
            if s < best_s:
                best_s = s
                best_b = b
                best_cp = cp2
                best_cn = cn2
                best_no = no
        if best_b is None:
            best_b = 1.0 if ones < N else 0.0
            best_cp = cp
            best_cn = cn
            best_no = ones
        h[p] = best_b
        cp = best_cp
        cn = best_cn
        ones = int(best_no)
    return h


def beam_h(n, width, deadline):
    N = n // 2
    states = [(np.zeros(n, dtype=float), np.zeros(n, dtype=float),
               np.zeros(n, dtype=float), 0)]
    for p in range(n):
        cand = []
        rem = n - p - 1
        for hst, cp, cn, ons in states:
            prev = hst[:p][::-1] if p > 0 else np.zeros(0)
            for b in (0.0, 1.0):
                no = ons + int(b)
                if no > N or no + rem < N:
                    continue
                h2 = hst.copy()
                h2[p] = b
                cp2 = cp.copy()
                cn2 = cn.copy()
                if p > 0:
                    if b > 0.5:
                        cn2[1:p + 1] += 1.0 - prev
                    else:
                        cp2[1:p + 1] += prev
                m = max(float(cp2[:p + 1].max()), float(cn2[:p + 1].max()))
                acts = int(np.count_nonzero(cp2[:p + 1] >= m - 0.5)) + int(
                    np.count_nonzero(cn2[:p + 1] >= m - 0.5))
                bias = 0.05 * abs(no - N * float(p + 1) / float(n))
                cand.append((h2, cp2, cn2, no, m + 0.01 * acts + bias))
        if not cand:
            return None, float("inf")
        if len(cand) > width:
            sc = np.array([z[4] for z in cand], dtype=float)
            idx = np.argpartition(sc, width - 1)[:width]
            cand = [cand[i] for i in idx]
        states = [(z[0], z[1], z[2], z[3]) for z in cand]
        if time.time() > deadline:
            return None, float("inf")
    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    for z in states:
        hh = z[0]
        cc = c5_of(hh, n)
        if cc < best_c5:
            best_c5 = cc
            best_h = hh.copy()
    return best_h, best_c5


def h_candidate(n, kind, sd, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if kind == 0:
        h = np.sin(math.pi * i / float(n)) ** 2
        return proj(h, n)
    if kind == 1:
        t = i / max(1.0, float(n - 1))
        p = 1 + (sd % 3)
        f = t ** float(p)
    elif kind == 2:
        q = 1 + (sd % 5)
        ph = 0.5 * math.pi * ((sd // 5) % 4)
        f = np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(q) * i / float(n) + ph)
    elif kind == 3:
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.RandomState(int(sd) + 1)
        f = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for q in range(1, 4):
            f += rng.normal() * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(q) * i / float(n) +
                                        rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    elif kind == 4:
        f = np.cos(math.pi * i / float(n))
    elif kind == 5:
        f = np.sin(math.pi * i / float(n))
    elif kind == 6:
        f = np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n))
        f = f * f
    else:
        f = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    f = f - np.mean(f)
    sc = float(np.max(np.abs(f))) if f.size else 0.0
    if sc > 1e-14:
        if rng is None:
            amp = 0.75
        else:
            amp = 0.45 + 0.55 * rng.rand()
        f = f / sc * amp
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * f
    return proj(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 500
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)
    h0 = proj(h0, n0)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = float(c5_of(best_h, best_n))

    for v in (0.5 * (h0 + h0[::-1]), 0.5 * (h0 + (1.0 - h0[::-1]))):
        vv = proj(v, best_n)
        cv = float(c5_of(vv, best_n))
        if cv < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = vv, cv

    cns = [best_n, 500, 600, 400, 320, 256]
    for nt in cns:
        if nt < 50 or nt > 800:
            continue
        for kind in range(7):
            for sd in range(2):
                if time.time() > t0 + 60.0:
                    break
                hc = h_candidate(int(nt), kind, sd, rng)
                cv = float(c5_of(hc, int(nt)))
                if cv < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hc, cv, int(nt)

    small_end = min(deadline - max(600, 0.6 * budget_s), t0 + min(180, 0.2 * budget_s))
    if time.time() < small_end:
        for nt in (32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 80, 96, 112, 128):
            if time.time() > small_end:
                break
            for _ in range(2):
                hg = greedy_h(nt, rng)
                cv = float(c5_of(hg, nt))
                if cv < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hg, cv, nt
            width = 550 if nt <= 48 else (320 if nt <= 64 else 160)
            hb, cb = beam_h(nt, width, min(time.time() + 12.0, small_end))
            if hb is not None and cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, nt
            if nt % 2 == 0 and best_n == nt and time.time() < small_end - 5.0:
                hb2, cb2, _ = binary_scan(hb, nt, min(time.time() + 6.0, small_end),
                                           max_passes=2, top=80)
                if hb2 is not None and cb2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hb2, cb2

    cont_end = min(deadline - max(450, 0.45 * budget_s),
                   time.time() + min(220, 0.25 * budget_s))
    if time.time() < cont_end and best_n <= 800:
        if best_n <= 700:
            h1, c1 = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, [1000, 10000, 100000], 60,
                                  max(t0 + 30.0, cont_end - 90.0))
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        h1, c1 = coord_cd(best_h, best_n, cont_end, max_passes=5, grid=34, refine=12)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    bin_end = min(deadline - max(220, 0.25 * budget_s),
                  time.time() + min(460, 0.45 * budget_s))
    nb = best_n if best_n % 2 == 0 else (best_n - 1 if best_n > 2 else best_n + 1)
    if nb % 2 == 0 and 60 <= nb <= 700 and time.time() < bin_end:
        start = transfer_h(best_h, best_n, nb) if nb != best_n else best_h
        hb, cb, _ = binary_optimize(start, nb, rng, bin_end)
        if hb is not None and cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, nb
            if time.time() < bin_end - 20.0:
                h1, c1 = coord_cd(best_h, best_n, bin_end, max_passes=3, grid=30, refine=10)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    res_end = deadline - 140.0
    if time.time() < res_end:
        tried = {best_n}
        for nt in (best_n, 500, 512, 600, 480, 400, 350, 320, 256):
            if time.time() > res_end:
                break
            nt = int(nt)
            if nt in tried or nt < 100 or nt > 800 or nt % 2:
                continue
            tried.add(nt)
            ht = transfer_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
            tl = min(time.time() + 45.0, res_end - 80.0)
            h1, c1 = coord_cd(ht, nt, tl, max_passes=2, grid=26, refine=9)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, nt
            if time.time() < res_end - 40.0:
                hb2, cb2, _ = binary_scan(ht, nt, min(time.time() + 35.0, res_end - 40.0),
                                           max_passes=4, top=55)
                if hb2 is not None and cb2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hb2, cb2, nt

    final_end = deadline - 8.0
    if best_n <= 800 and time.time() < final_end:
        if best_n <= 700 and time.time() < final_end - 90.0:
            h1, c1 = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, [20000, 200000], 35, final_end - 90.0)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        h1, c1 = coord_cd(best_h, best_n, final_end - 30.0, max_passes=3, grid=38, refine=13)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < final_end - 10.0:
            hb2, cb2, _ = binary_scan(best_h, best_n, final_end, max_passes=2, top=45)
            if hb2 is not None and cb2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hb2, cb2
        h1, c1 = coord_cd(best_h, best_n, final_end, max_passes=2, grid=32, refine=10)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    fh = proj(best_h, best_n)
    fc = float(c5_of(fh, best_n))
    return fh, fc, int(best_n)
```
